def convolve_nonneg(a):
    n=len(a); out_len=2*n-1; size=1 << (out_len-1).bit_length(); fa=fft(a,size); b=ifft(fa*fa).real[:out_len]; return np.maximum(b,0)
```

Gradient for smooth objective:

beta = ...
c = conv(p,p)
# logsumexp stable:
m = np.max(c)
exp_c = np.exp(beta*(c-m))
s = exp_c / exp_c.sum()
mavg = np.dot(s,c)
h = corr(p,s)
grad = p * (h - mavg)  # up to positive constant 4n; can scale

Mirror descent implementation for full:
```
def mirror_step(z, eta, beta):
    p=softmax(z)
    c=conv(p,p)
    s=softmax(beta*c)
    h=corr(p,s)
    avg=np.dot(s,c)
    # update z <- z - eta * p*(h-avg)? Wait z update gradient = const * p*(h-avg). Natural update: z_new = z - step*(h-avg) (since log p change = -step*(h-avg)).
    z = z - step*(h-avg)
    z -= np.mean(z)
```
We need choose step. h-avg scale ~p? p ~1/n, h ~sum p over lags ~1/n? Scale ~1/n². Step must be n? Let's estimate: p ~1/n, c~1/n, s uniform? h~1/n, avg~1/n, diff~1/n². To change z O(1), step ~n². That's large. But if we use gradient p*(h-avg), scale p*diff ~1/n³; step ~n³. Better use direct h-avg in log p update: z_i -= alpha*(h_i-avg). Since h_i ~1/n? Wait h=∑s p_{k-i}; s sum1, p ~1/n -> h ~1/n. avg=∑s c ~1/n. diff maybe 1/n? Actually variation of h maybe 1/n? For uniform c nonuniform, h variation ~1/n. To change z O(1), alpha O(n). Let's test: p_i=1/n, h uniform if s symmetric? If s central-heavy, h_i central? h_i ~ sum s_k (1/n)=1/n; variation due to s shape maybe s central creates h central? h_i = correlation of p with s; if s peaked at k=n, h_i ~0.5/n; difference small. But with nonuniform p, diff scale maybe p amplitude. Hard.

We can make update scale-invariant: Let d = h - avg. Multiply by n: update z -= alpha * (n*d). Choose alpha ~0.01-0.1. Or normalize d by its norm: z -= alpha * d / (np.std(d)+eps). Then step O(1). Adam handles.

Let's design a polished algorithm:
1. Helper functions: `clip_nonneg`, `normalize_sum`, `convolve_fft`, `score_from_conv`, `softmax`, `smooth_state` returns c,s,h,grad.
2. `opt_l_bfgs(z, m, budget, beta_factor=5, maxiter=...)`: Use scipy optimize. But if optimizing z with first coordinate fixed, y length m-1. Objective function uses global? No closures allowed, so helpers top-level taking parameters. But objective inside minimize needs data; if we define nested function? Rule says no closures from function nesting; lambdas no.        g = g - np.mean(g)
       # maybe scale g by n? We'll use Adam on g * n? Let's use g_scaled = g * n.

       z -= z.mean()

Helper functions:
```
def _next_pow2(m): return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length() if m>1 else 1

def _fft_conv(a):
   n=len(a); out=2*n-1; N=_next_pow2(out)
   fa=fft(a, N); b=ifft(fa*fa).real[:out]
   return np.maximum(b, 0.0)  # remove tiny negatives
```
Maybe using `scipy.fft.fft` with `workers=-1`? single CPU; no.

`_fft_corr_p_s(p,s)` returns h length len(p):
```
def _corr_p_s(p, s):
   n=len(p); N=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
   fp=fft(p,N); fs=fft(s,N)
   return ifft(conj(fp)*fs).real[:n]
```
Need ensure no wrap: N next pow2(2n-1) exactly. Good.

`_softmax(z)`:
```
def _softmax(z):
   z = z - np.max(z)
   e = np.exp(z)
   s=e.sum()
   if s==0 or not finite: return np.full_like(z,1.0/len(z))
   return e/s
```
For stability, if z huge range, clip to [-50,50] before? Let zmax=max; z-zmax ≤0. Minimum maybe -1000 underflows to 0 okay. If all -inf? no.

`_fast_score_p(p)`:
```
c=_fft_conv(p); return 2*len(p)*float(np.max(c))
```
If sum not 1? If p not normalized, formula should be `2n max conv(p)/(sum p)^2`. For internal, we normalize to sum1. But when evaluating candidates from z, p sum1. Use sum1. For parent, normalize p to sum1 before z. If computing fast score for arbitrary a, use general:
```
def _fast_score_general(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.clip(a,0,None)
   S=a.sum(); if S<0.01: return inf
   c=_fft_conv(a)
   return 2*len(a)*max(c)/(S*S)
```
This includes clipping? Evaluate also clips to 1000; for internal we keep <=1000. For normalize, maybe sum=1. Use both.

`_fast_score_z(z)`:
```
p=_softmax(z); return _fast_score_p(p)
```

`_smooth_score_z(z,beta)`:
```
p=_softmax(z); c=_fft_conv(p); cmax=c.max(); if cmax<=0 return 0
shift=beta*(c-cmax)
# beta might large, but shift max 0. sumexp at least1.
logsum = cmax + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(shift)))/beta
return 2*len(p)*logsum
```
If beta=0? return 2n*cmax.

`_smooth_grad_z(z,beta)`:
```
p=_softmax(z); n=len(p)
c=_fft_conv(p)
cmax=c.max()
shift=beta*(c-cmax); e=np.exp(shift); s=e/e.sum()
avg=float(np.dot(s,c))
h=_corr_p_s(p,s)
g = 4.0*n*p*(h-avg)
g -= g.mean()? The full gradient sum should be zero; due numeric, subtract mean to maintain scale invariance (with anchor fixed not necessary but okay). Return g.
```

For objective/gradient wrappers:

def _z_from_y(y, n, anchor):
    z=np.empty(n)
    if anchor>0: z[:anchor]=y[:anchor]
    z[anchor]=0.0
    if anchor+1<n: z[anchor+1:]=y[anchor:]

```
def _grad_from_z(g, n, anchor):
    out=np.empty(n-1)
    if anchor>0: out[:anchor]=g[:anchor]
    if anchor+1<n: out[anchor:]=g[anchor+1:]
    return out
```

`_choose_anchor(z)`:

p=_softmax(z); logp=np.log(p+1e-300); med=np.median(logp); return int(np.argmin(np.abs(logp-med)))
```

```
def _check_deadline(deadline):
    if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
        raise _Timeout
```
In objective:
```
def _z_objective(y, n, anchor, beta, deadline):
    _check_deadline(deadline)
    z=_z_from_y(y,n,anchor)
    return _smooth_score_z(z, beta)
```

Tracker class for callbacks to update best and raise timeout:
```
class _LbfgsTracker:
    def __init__(self,n,anchor,deadline,best_container):
       self.n=n; self.anchor=anchor; self.deadline=deadline; self.best_container=best_container
    def __call__(self, y):
       _check_deadline(self.deadline)
       z=_z_from_y(...)
       sc=_fast_score_z(z)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < self.best_container['score']:
           self.best_container['score']=sc
           self.best_container['z']=z.copy()

Natural Adam refinement:
```
def _adam_refine(z, deadline, lr=0.05, beta1=0.9, beta2=0.999, eps=1e-8, beta_factor=5.0, max_iter=None, label=''):
    n=len(z)
    z=z - z.mean()
    best_z=z.copy(); best_score=_fast_score_z(z)
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n)
    t=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       p=_softmax(z)
       c=_fft_conv(p)
       score=2*n*max(c)
       if score<best_score: update
       cmax=c.max(); beta=beta_factor/max(cmax,1e-15)
       s=_softmax_c(c,beta) # exp(beta*(c-cmax))
       h=_corr_p_s(p,s)
       g=h - float(np.dot(s,c))
       g = np.nan_to_num(g)
       g *= n  # scale to O(1)
       g -= g.mean()
       m = beta1*m + (1-beta1)*g
       v = beta2*v + (1-beta2)*g*g
       bc=m/(1-beta1**(t+1)); vc=v/(1-beta2**(t+1))
       z = z - lr * bc/(sqrt(vc)+eps)
       z -= z.mean()
       z=np.clip(z,-30,30)
       t+=1
       if t%50==0: maybe no-improve check; if no improve for 2000, break/restart
    return best_z, best_score
```
But beta_factor/cmax: If cmax~0.75/n, beta~6.67n, exp difference up to maybe beta*(cmax-min) ~6.67, good. If c has flat, s spread. `s` computation can reuse c. We already have c; define `_softmax_shift(c,beta)`.

The gradient direction `g=h - avg`. Should we subtract mean? It sums zero theoretically, but do. Scale by n. For n=5582, lr=0.05 yields z update maybe O(1) if Adam normalized. Good. But if g near zero, no.

Downsample parent to m:

```
def _resample(p, m):
   n=len(p)
   if n==m: return p.copy()
   if n > m:
      idx = (np.arange(m)*(n-1))/(m-1) if m>1 else 0
      # linear interpolation with fill
      return np.interp(np.linspace(0,m-1,m), np.linspace(0,n-1,n), p)
   else:
      # upsample
      x_old=np.linspace(0,n-1,n); x_new=np.linspace(0,m-1,m)
      return np.interp(x_new,x_old,p)
```

Now, let's verify gradient/correlation formula with small examples to avoid sign errors. Suppose p has high at center? Uniform: cmax central. s approx delta central. h_i = ∑ p_{i+k} s_k ~ p_i (if k central? Wait if s_k at k=n-1, h_j=∑ p_i s_{i+j}; for s at k=n-1, h_j = p_{n-1-j} if n-1-j valid. So h_j = p reversed! avg=∑s c ~ c_center. Gradient p*(h-avg) = p_j*(p_rev_j - cmax). This decreases p where its reversed partner is high, relative to avg. Good. Natural g=h-avg = p_rev-avg. This is subgradient of central peak (∂c_center/∂z =2p(p_rev-cm)). Yes.

`_softmax_c` for s:
```
def _softmax_weights(c, beta):
   cmax=np.max(c)
   w=np.exp(beta*(c-cmax))
   s=w.sum()
   if s<=0: return np.full_like(c,1/len(c))
   return w/s
```
If beta large and c-cmax max negative huge, exp underflow; at max=1. Good.

```
def _z_from_p(p, clip=30):
   p=np.asarray(p,float); p=np.maximum(p,1e-20); p=p/p.sum()
   z=np.log(p); z-=z.mean()
   med=np.median(z); anchor=int(np.argmin(np.abs(z-med)))
   z=z-z[anchor]
   z=np.clip(z, -clip, clip)
   return z
```

Need implement `_resample` for p arrays. For p start maybe parent_arr (not normalized). First normalize. For upsample, if old n=1, return full constant. Use `np.interp`; if new_m=1, return full sum at first. Ensure nonnegative.

```
def _resample(p, m):
    p=np.asarray(p,float).ravel()
    if len(p)==0: return np.ones(m)/m
    S=p.sum(); if S<=0: return np.ones(m)/m
    p=p/S
    n=len(p)
    if n==m: return p
    if m==1: return np.array([1.0])
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0, n-1, n)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0, m-1, m)
    q=np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    q=np.maximum(q,0); S=q.sum(); if S<=0: q=np.ones(m)/m
    return q/S
```
Linear interpolation may create small values; okay.

`_smooth_array`:
```
def _smooth_array(p, k):
   if k<=1: return p
   k=k if k%2==1 else k+1
   ker=np.ones(k)/k
   return np.convolve(p, ker, mode='same')
```
For p length <k, adjust.

```
def _make_profiles(parent_arr, n, rng):
   prof=[]
   if parent_arr is not None and parent_arr.size>0:
      p=_resample(parent_arr,n)
      prof.append(p)
      sm5=_smooth_array(p,5); if diff: prof.append(_normalize(sm5))
      sm15=...
   prof.append(np.ones(n)/n)
   # asymmetric step
   half=n//2
   for ratio in [0.7,1.4]:
      p=np.ones(n); p[:half]=ratio; p=_normalize(p); prof.append(p)
   # cos/sine
   i=np.arange(n)
   for f,amp in [(1,0.6),(2,0.5),(4,0.4)]:
      p=1+amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*i/n)
      p=np.maximum(p,0.01); prof.append(_normalize(p))
      p=1+amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*i/n)
      p=np.maximum(p,0.01); prof.append(_normalize(p))
   # ramp
   for slope in [-0.8,-0.4,0.4,0.8]:
      p=1+slope*i/(n-1)
      p=np.maximum(p,0.01); prof.append(_normalize(p))
   # random low freq (if rng)
   znoise=np.zeros(n)
   for f in [1,2,3,5,8]:
      amp=np.exp(-f/fmax)*0.8
      phase=rng.uniform(0,2π)
      znoise += amp*np.cos(2π f i/n + phase)
   p=np.exp(0.5*znoise); prof.append(normalize)
   # dedup? not necessary.
```

def _run_lbfgs(z0, deadline, maxiter=150, beta_factor=6.0, pgtol=1e-6, bound=15.0, tag='lbfgs'):
    n=len(z0)
    if n<=1: return z0.copy(), _fast_score_z(z0)
    p0=_softmax(z0); c0=_fft_conv(p0); sc0=_fast_score_p(p0)
    best_z=z0.copy(); best_score=sc0
    cmax0 = float(np.max(c0))
    beta = beta_factor / max(cmax0, 1e-15)
    # If beta too tiny? if cmax0 huge? ok.
    anchor = _choose_anchor(z0)
    y0=np.empty(n-1)
    if anchor>0: y0[:anchor]=np.clip(z0[:anchor], -bound,bound)
    if anchor+1<n: y0[anchor:]=np.clip(z0[anchor+1:], -bound,bound)
    # If z0 anchor not zero? y0 are relative to anchor? z0 maybe already zero at some other anchor; p same. But if we set z_anchor=0 and other z values as z0 (not subtract z0[anchor]), softmax changes! Need subtract z0[anchor] from all values before removing anchor. Because fixing z_anchor=0 requires relative z: z_i - z_anchor. Since z0 softmax invariant to constant, we can set anchor 0 by subtracting z0[anchor] from all z. So:
    zrel = z0 - z0[anchor]
    y0 from zrel excluding anchor; clip.
```

"""Coarse-to-fine L-BFGS + multiplicative (Adam) mirror descent for bounded self-convolution."""
import time
import numpy as np
try:
    from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
except Exception:
    from numpy.fft import fft, ifft
from scipy.optimize import minimize

class _Timeout(Exception): pass

def _next_pow2(m):
    m=int(max(1,m))
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
...
```
If scipy.fft not, numpy. But scipy allowed.

`fft` and `ifft` from numpy support `n` arg. Good. But numpy if `ifft` returns complex; okay. For `scipy.fft`, `ifft` too. No workers.

`_fft_conv`:
```
def _fft_conv(a):
    n=a.size
    out=2*n-1
    N=_next_pow2(out)
    fa=fft(a,N)
    b=ifft(fa*fa).real[:out]
    np.maximum(b, 0.0, out=b)
    return b
```
Use `out=b` for in-place.

`_fft_corr`:
```
def _fft_corr(p,s):
    n=p.size
    N=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    fp=fft(p,N)
    fs=fft(s,N)
    return ifft(np.conj(fp)*fs).real[:n]
```
Could real have tiny imaginary; fine.

`_softmax`:
```
def _softmax(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,float)
    z=z - np.max(z)
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
       return np.full_like(z, 1.0/len(z))
    return e/s
```
If z contains nan, max nan. Use `z=np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)`. Then max.

`_normalize_to_sum`:
```
def _normalize_to_sum(a, target=1.0):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       return np.full(a.shape, target/max(1,len(a)))
    return a*(target/s)
```

`_fast_score_p`:
```
def _fast_score_p(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,float).ravel()
    if p.size==0: return np.inf
    s=p.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return np.inf
    p=p/s
    c=_fft_conv(p)
    return float(2*p.size*np.max(c))
```
General with sum? Since we always normalize; but if p arbitrary, we normalize. That changes score? Score invariant so okay. If p all zero, inf.

`_fast_score_z`: p=softmax; return _fast_score_p(p) (normalizes again maybe redundant).

`_smooth_score_z`:
```
def _smooth_score_z(z,beta):
    p=_softmax(z)
    c=_fft_conv(p)
    n=p.size
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    if cmax<=0: return 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(beta) or beta <=0: return 2.0*n*cmax
    shift=beta*(c-cmax)
    # clip shift to [-700,0] to avoid under/overflow
    shift=np.maximum(np.minimum(shift,0.0), -700.0)
    se=np.sum(np.exp(shift))
    if se<=0 or not finite: return 2n*cmax
    return float(2*n*(cmax + np.log(se)/beta))
```
`np.exp(0)=1`, se≥1. Good.

`_smooth_grad_z(z,beta)`: as above. Use shift clip.
```
def _softmax_weights_c(c,beta):
   cmax=max(c)
   if beta <=0? return uniform
   shift=np.clip(beta*(c-cmax), -700, 0)
   e=np.exp(shift); s=e.sum(); if s<=0: uniform
   return e/s
```
Then h.

Now, `_adam_refine` implementation details:
```
def _adam_refine(z0, deadline, lr=0.05, beta1=0.9, beta2=0.999, eps=1e-8, beta_factor=5.0, seed=None):
    n=len(z0)
    if n<=1 or time.monotonic()>=deadline: return z0.copy(), _fast_score_z(z0)
    z=np.asarray(z0,float).copy(); z-=np.mean(z); z=np.clip(z,-25,25)
    best_z=z.copy(); best_score=_fast_score_z(z)
    p=_softmax(z) maybe compute in loop
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t=0; no_improve=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       p=_softmax(z)
       c=_fft_conv(p)
       score=float(2*n*np.max(c))
       if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score - 1e-12:
           best_score=score; best_z=z.copy(); no_improve=0
       else:
           no_improve += 1
       if no_improve > 1500:
           scale = 0.05 + 0.1*(no_improve/1500.0)
           z = best_z + scale*rng.standard_normal(n)
           z -= np.mean(z); z=np.clip(z,-25,25)
           m.fill(0); v.fill(0); no_improve=0
           continue
       cmax=float(np.max(c))
       beta=beta_factor/max(cmax,1e-15)
       s=_softmax_weights_c(c,beta)
       h=_fft_corr(p,s)
       avg=float(np.dot(s,c))
       g=h-avg
       g=np.nan_to_num(g)
       g *= n
       g -= np.mean(g)
       t += 1
       m = beta1*m + (1-beta1)*g
       v = beta2*v + (1-beta2)*(g*g)
       # bias correction maybe if t<10000:
       if t > 200:
           mm=m; vv=v
       else:
           mm=m/(1-beta1**t)
           vv=v/(1-beta2**t)
       z = z - lr * mm/(np.sqrt(vv)+eps)
       z -= np.mean(z)
       z=np.clip(z,-25,25)
    return best_z,best_score
```
Potential performance: `beta1**t` each iter until 200; okay. For large t, skip.

But if no_improve >1500, step scale huge? For a 300s chunk, iterations maybe 5000, restarts okay.

Could adapt lr: if score increasing often? We don't track. Use small lr 0.03 default. In propose, pass lr values.

Now, coarse-to-fine path:
```
def _coarse_to_fine_path(p_start, final_n, deadline, levels=(64,128,256,512,1024,2048), per_s=25.0, beta_factor=4.0, tag='ctf'):
    p = _normalize_to_sum(p_start)
    m0=levels[0]
    p = _resample(p, m0)
    best_final_z=None; best_final_score=np.inf
    for m in levels:
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
       p = _resample(p, m)
       z = _z_from_p(p)
       sub_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + per_s)
       z, sc = _run_lbfgs(z, sub_deadline, maxiter=80, beta_factor=beta_factor, tag=f"ctf_{m}")
       p = _normalize_to_sum(_softmax(z))
       # Save final candidate by upsampling current p
       if final_n not in levels or m == final_n? We can at each level upsample to final_n and update path best.
       p_final = _resample(p, final_n)
       zf = _z_from_p(_normalize_to_sum(p_final))
       sf = _fast_score_z(zf)
       if sf < best_final_score:
           best_final_score=sf; best_final_z=zf
    if best_final_z is None:
       p_final=_resample(p, final_n); best_final_z=_z_from_p(_normalize_to_sum(p_final)); best_final_score=_fast_score_z(best_final_z)
    return best_final_z,best_final_score
```
Running L-BFGS at each level may use most of per_s; if 6 levels*25=150s. Good. If deadline shorter, break.

# remove duplicates, sort

Need define `_LbfgsTracker` before `_run_lbfgs`. It uses `_fast_score_z`. No closure.

```
class _LbfgsTracker:
    def __init__(self, n, anchor, deadline, container, eval_mod):
        self.n=n; ...; self.counter=0
    def __call__(self, x):
        _check_deadline(self.deadline)
        self.counter +=1
        if self.counter % self.eval_mod == 0 or self.counter==1:
            z=_z_from_y(x,self.n,self.anchor)
            sc=_fast_score_z(z)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < self.container[1]:
                self.container[0]=z.copy(); self.container[1]=sc
```

Now, `_z_from_y` must handle y maybe list. Use np.asarray.

```
def _z_from_y(y,n,anchor):
    y=np.asarray(y,float)
    z=np.empty(n)
    if anchor>0: z[:anchor]=y[:anchor]
    z[anchor]=0.0
    if anchor+1<n: z[anchor+1:]=y[anchor:]
    return z
```
If y length short, error; but solver correct. For n=1, y length0; but not used.

`_remove_anchor`:
```
def _remove_anchor(g,n,anchor):
    g=np.asarray(g,float)
    out=np.empty(n-1)
    if anchor>0: out[:anchor]=g[:anchor]
    if anchor+1<n: out[anchor:]=g[anchor+1:]
    return out
```

def _push_family(family, z, sc, max_keep=12):
    if z is None or not np.isfinite(sc): return family

        del family[max_keep:]
    return family

```
def _fft_conv(a):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <=0: return np.array([], dtype=float)
    out_len=2*n-1
    N=_next_pow2(out_len)
    fa=fft(a.astype(np.float64, copy=False), N)
    b=ifft(fa*fa).real[:out_len]
    np.maximum(b,0.0,out=b)
    return b
```

def _run_lbfgs(z0, deadline, maxiter=100, beta_factor=6.0, pgtol=1e-7, bound=15.0):
    z0=np.asarray(z0,float)
    n=z0.size
    z0=np.nan_to_num(z0,...)
    p0=_softmax(z0)
    sc0=_score_unit_p(p0)
    best_z=z0.copy(); best_score=sc0
    if n<=1 or time.monotonic()>=deadline: return best_z,best_score
    cmax0 = float(np.max(_fft_conv(p0))) # could reuse? To avoid second, compute c0
    c0=_fft_conv(p0); cmax0=max
    beta=beta_factor/max(cmax0,1e-15)
    anchor=_choose_anchor(z0)
    zrel=z0 - z0[anchor]
    y0=np.empty(n-1)
    ...
    container=[None, np.inf]
    eval_mod = 1 if n<=512 else 2
    tracker=_LbfgsTracker(n, anchor, deadline, container, eval_mod)
    bounds=[(-bound, bound)]*(n-1)
    if minimize is None: return best_z,best_score
    options=...
    try: res=minimize(...)
    except _Timeout: res=None
    except Exception: res=None
    if container[0] is not None and container[1] < best_score: update
    if res is not None:
       x=np.asarray(res.x,float)
       if x.size==n-1:
          zres=_z_from_y(x,n,anchor); scres=_fast_score_z(zres); update
    return best_z,best_score
```

```
def _adam_refine(z0, deadline, lr=0.05, beta1=0.9, beta2=0.999, eps=1e-8, beta_factor=5.0, seed=None):
    z=np.asarray(z0,float).copy(); z=np.nan_to_num...
    z-=np.mean(z); z=np.clip(z,-25,25)
    best_z=z.copy(); best_score=_fast_score_z(z)
    if z.size<=1 or time>=deadline: return
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0; no_improve=0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       p=_softmax(z); c=_fft_conv(p); sc=float(2*n*np.max(c))
       if ...
       if no_improve>2000: restart
       beta=beta_factor/max(float(np.max(c)),1e-15)
       s=_softmax_weights_c(c,beta)
       h=_fft_corr(p,s)
       avg=float(np.dot(s,c))
       g=h-avg; g=nan_to_num; g *= n; g-=g.mean()
       t+=1; m=...; v=...
       if t<200: bc=... vc=...
       else: bc=m vc=v
       z = z - lr*bc/(np.sqrt(vc)+eps)
       z-=z.mean(); z=np.clip(z,-25,25)
    return best_z,best_score
```
If `no_improve` restart, we should reset best? best_z stores best. Use `z=best_z + scale*rng.normal`. Good.

Potential restart scale grows: `scale=0.05 + 0.1*min(1.0, no_improve/5000.0)`. fine.

def _coarse_to_fine_path(p_start, final_n, deadline, levels=(64,128,256,512,1024,2048), per_s=22.0, beta_factor=4.0, tag='ctf'):
   p=_normalize_to_sum(p_start)
   if p.size==0: p=np.ones(levels[0])
   all_levels=[]
   for m in levels:
      if m < final_n: all_levels.append(int(m))
   all_levels.append(int(final_n))
   # unique sorted
   seen=set(); myst=[]
   for m in sorted(all_levels):
      if m>=2 and m not in seen: seen.add(m); myst.append(m)
   p=_resample(p, myst[0])
   best_z=None; best_score=np.inf
   for m in myst:
      if time.monotonic()>=deadline: break
      p=_resample(p, m)
      z=_z_from_p(p)
      sub=min(deadline, time.monotonic()+per_s)
      z,sc=_run_lbfgs(z, sub, maxiter=80, beta_factor=beta_factor)
      p=_normalize_to_sum(_softmax(z))
      p_final=_resample(p, final_n)
      zf=_z_from_p(p_final)
      sf=_fast_score_z(zf)
      if sf < best_score: best_score=sf; best_z=zf.copy()
   if best_z is None: ...
   return best_z,best_score
```

```python
"""
Strategy summary
----------------
The score is
    2n * max(a * a) / (sum a)^2
where a * a is the ordinary linear convolution.  It is scale invariant, so
all internal optimization is done on the simplex sum(a)=1.

This program uses a coarse-to-fine second-order search:
  1. Start from the supplied parent sequence if available, plus generated
     asymmetric profiles.
  2. Optimize a smoothed max-convolution objective at low resolutions using
     L-BFGS-B, then upsampling.
  3. Refine at full resolution with alternating L-BFGS-B and short Adam tuned
     mirror-descent steps.
  4. Verify a short set of finalists with the real evaluate_sequence() and
     return the best valid list.

The smoothed objective is
    2n * (1/beta) * log(sum_k exp(beta c_k)),
with c = conv(p,p).  Its gradient in the log-probability variables is
    4n p_i ( (s * p)_i - sum_k s_k c_k ),
where s_k is a softmax over the active peaks.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
except Exception:
    from numpy.fft import fft, ifft

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
except Exception:
    minimize = None


class _Timeout(Exception):
    pass


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _fft_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    out_len = 2 * n - 1
    nfft = _next_pow2(out_len)
    fa = fft(a, nfft)
    out = ifft(fa * fa).real[:out_len]
    np.maximum(out, 0.0, out=out)
    return out


def _fft_corr_p_s(p, s):
    """
    Return h[j] = sum_i p[i] s[i+j] for j=0..n-1.
    This is the cross-correlation needed for the smoothed gradient.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    s = np.asarray(s, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    nfft = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    fp = fft(p, nfft)
    fs = fft(s, nfft)
    return ifft(np.conj(fp) * fs).real[:n]


def _softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    z = z - np.max(z)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(z.shape, 1.0 / z.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _normalize_to_sum(a, target=1.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(a.shape, target / a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return (target / s) * a


def _score_unit_p(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    c = _fft_conv(p)
    return float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))


def _fast_score_p(p):
    p = _normalize_to_sum(p, 1.0)
    return _score_unit_p(p)


def _fast_score_z(z):
    p = _softmax(z)
    return _score_unit_p(p)


def _softmax_weights_c(c, beta):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = c.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if cmax <= 0.0:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / m, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.isfinite(beta) or beta <= 0.0:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / m, dtype=np.float64)
    shift = beta * (c - cmax)
    shift = np.clip(shift, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(shift)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / m, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _smooth_score_z(z, beta):
    p = _softmax(z)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0
    c = _fft_conv(p)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if cmax <= 0.0:
        return 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(beta) or beta <= 0.0:
        return float(2.0 * n * cmax)
    shift = np.clip(beta * (c - cmax), -700.0, 0.0)
    se = float(np.sum(np.exp(shift)))
    if not np.isfinite(se) or se <= 0.0:
        return float(2.0 * n * cmax)
    return float(2.0 * n * (cmax + np.log(se) / beta))


def _smooth_grad_z(z, beta):
    p = _softmax(z)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    c = _fft_conv(p)
    s = _softmax_weights_c(c, beta)
    avg = float(np.dot(s, c))
    h = _fft_corr_p_s(p, s)
    g = 4.0 * n * p * (h - avg)
    g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    g -= float(np.mean(g))
    return g


def _check_deadline(deadline):
    if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > float(deadline):
        raise _Timeout()


def _z_from_y(y, n, anchor):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    z = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if anchor > 0:
        z[:anchor] = y[:anchor]
    z[anchor] = 0.0
    if anchor + 1 < n:
        z[anchor + 1:] = y[anchor:]
    return z


def _remove_anchor(z, n, anchor):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out = np.empty(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    if anchor > 0:
        out[:anchor] = z[:anchor]
    if anchor + 1 < n:
        out[anchor:] = z[anchor + 1:]
    return out


def _choose_anchor(z):
    n = z.size
    if n <= 1:
        return 0
    p = _softmax(z)
    lp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    med = float(np.median(lp))
    return int(np.argmin(np.abs(lp - med)))


def _z_objective(y, n, anchor, beta, deadline):
    _check_deadline(deadline)
    if np.asarray(y).size != n - 1:
        return np.inf
    z = _z_from_y(y, n, anchor)
    val = _smooth_score_z(z, beta)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return 1e100
    return float(val)


def _z_gradient(y, n, anchor, beta, deadline):
    _check_deadline(deadline)
    if np.asarray(y).size != n - 1:
        return np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    z = _z_from_y(y, n, anchor)
    g = _smooth_grad_z(z, beta)
    return _remove_anchor(g, n, anchor)


class _LbfgsTracker:
    def __init__(self, n, anchor, deadline, container, eval_mod):
        self.n = int(n)
        self.anchor = int(anchor)
        self.deadline = float(deadline)
        self.container = container
        self.eval_mod = max(1, int(eval_mod))
        self.count = 0

    def __call__(self, y):
        _check_deadline(self.deadline)
        self.count += 1
        if self.count != 1 and (self.count % self.eval_mod) != 0:
            return
        if np.asarray(y).size != self.n - 1:
            return
        z = _z_from_y(y, self.n, self.anchor)
        sc = _fast_score_z(z)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < self.container[1]:
            self.container[0] = z.copy()
            self.container[1] = float(sc)


def _run_lbfgs(z0, deadline, maxiter=100, beta_factor=6.0, pgtol=1e-7, bound=15.0):
    z0 = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(z0, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    n = z0.size
    if n <= 1:
        return z0.copy(), _fast_score_z(z0)

    p0 = _softmax(z0)
    sc0 = _score_unit_p(p0)
    best_z = z0.copy()
    best_sc = sc0

    if time.monotonic() >= float(deadline):
        return best_z, best_sc
    if minimize is None:
        return best_z, best_sc

    c0 = _fft_conv(p0)
    cmax0 = float(np.max(c0))
    beta = float(beta_factor) / max(cmax0, 1e-15)

    anchor = _choose_anchor(z0)
    zrel = z0 - z0[anchor]
    y0 = np.empty(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    if anchor > 0:
        y0[:anchor] = np.clip(zrel[:anchor], -bound, bound)
    if anchor + 1 < n:
        y0[anchor:] = np.clip(zrel[anchor + 1:], -bound, bound)

    container = [None, np.inf]
    eval_mod = 1 if n <= 512 else 2
    tracker = _LbfgsTracker(n, anchor, deadline, container, eval_mod)

    bounds = [(-bound, bound)] * (n - 1)
    options = {
        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
        "ftol": 1e-12,
        "pgtol": float(pgtol),
        "disp": False,
        "maxls": 20,
        "m": 8 if n > 1000 else 12,
    }

    try:
        res = minimize(
            _z_objective,
            y0,
            jac=_z_gradient,
            args=(n, anchor, beta, deadline),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            bounds=bounds,
            options=options,
            callback=tracker,
        )
    except _Timeout:
        res = None
    except Exception:
        res = None

    if container[0] is not None and container[1] < best_sc:
        best_z = container[0].copy()
        best_sc = float(container[1])

    if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
        x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        if x.size == n - 1:
            zres = _z_from_y(x, n, anchor)
            scres = _fast_score_z(zres)
            if np.isfinite(scres) and scres < best_sc:
                best_z = zres.copy()
                best_sc = float(scres)

    return best_z, best_sc


def _adam_refine(z0, deadline, lr=0.05, beta1=0.9, beta2=0.999, eps=1e-8,
                 beta_factor=5.0, seed=None):
    z = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(z0, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    z = np.clip(z, -25.0, 25.0)
    n = z.size
    if n <= 1:
        return z.copy(), _fast_score_z(z)

    best_z = z.copy()
    best_sc = _fast_score_z(z)
    if time.monotonic() >= float(deadline):
        return best_z, best_sc

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    no_improve = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    while time.monotonic() < float(deadline):
        p = _softmax(z)
        c = _fft_conv(p)
        sc = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = sc
            best_z = z.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 2000:
            scale = 0.05 + 0.10 * min(1.0, no_improve / 5000.0)
            z = best_z + scale * rng.standard_normal(n)
            z -= float(np.mean(z))
            z = np.clip(z, -25.0, 25.0)
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            no_improve = 0
            continue

        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        beta = float(beta_factor) / max(cmax, 1e-15)
        s = _softmax_weights_c(c, beta)
        h = _fft_corr_p_s(p, s)
        avg = float(np.dot(s, c))
        g = h - avg
        g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        g *= n
        g -= float(np.mean(g))

        t += 1
        m = beta1 * m + (1.0 - beta1) * g
        v = beta2 * v + (1.0 - beta2) * (g * g)

        if t < 200:
            mm = m / (1.0 - beta1 ** t)
            vv = v / (1.0 - beta2 ** t)
        else:
            mm = m
            vv = v

        z = z - float(lr) * mm / (np.sqrt(vv) + eps)
        z -= float(np.mean(z))
        z = np.clip(z, -25.0, 25.0)

    return best_z, best_sc


def _resample(p, m):
    p = _normalize_to_sum(p, 1.0)
    m = int(max(1, m))
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / m, dtype=np.float64)
    if m == 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if n == m:
        return p.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, n - 1, n)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, m - 1, m)
    q = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    return _normalize_to_sum(q, 1.0)


def _smooth_array(p, k):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0 or k <= 1:
        return p.copy()
    k = int(k)
    if k % 2 == 0:
        k += 1
    ker = np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64) / float(k)
    return np.convolve(p, ker, mode="same")


def _z_from_p(p):
    p = _normalize_to_sum(p, 1.0)
    z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-20))
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    return z


def _make_profiles(parent_arr, n, rng):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    prof = []

    if parent_arr is not None:
        p = _resample(parent_arr, n)
        prof.append(p)
        sm5 = _smooth_array(p, 5)
        prof.append(_normalize_to_sum(sm5, 1.0))
        sm11 = _smooth_array(p, 11)
        prof.append(_normalize_to_sum(sm11, 1.0))
        if n >= 256:
            sm25 = _smooth_array(p, 25)
            prof.append(_normalize_to_sum(sm25, 1.0))

    prof.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))

    if n >= 3:
        h = n // 2
        for ratio in (0.55, 1.0, 1.6, 2.2):
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            p[:h] = 0.55
            p[h:] = ratio
            prof.append(_normalize_to_sum(p, 1.0))

    if n >= 3:
        t = n // 3
        for (a, b, c) in ((2.0, 1.0, 0.35), (1.0, 2.0, 0.35),
                          (0.4, 1.0, 2.0), (1.8, 0.6, 1.0)):
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            p[:t] = a
            p[t:2 * t] = b
            p[2 * t:] = c
            prof.append(_normalize_to_sum(p, 1.0))

    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for slope in (-0.9, -0.45, 0.45, 0.9):
        p = 1.0 + slope * idx / float(max(1, n - 1))
        p = np.maximum(p, 0.05)
        prof.append(_normalize_to_sum(p, 1.0))

    for f, amp in ((1, 0.65), (2, 0.55), (4, 0.45), (8, 0.35)):
        base = 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * idx / float(n))
        prof.append(_normalize_to_sum(np.maximum(base, 0.05), 1.0))
        base = 1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * idx / float(n))
        prof.append(_normalize_to_sum(np.maximum(base, 0.05), 1.0))

    base = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for f in (1, 2, 3, 5, 8, 13):
        amp = 0.60 / np.sqrt(float(f))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        base += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * idx / float(n) + phase)
    base = np.maximum(base, 0.05)
    prof.append(_normalize_to_sum(base, 1.0))

    return prof


def _element_score(item):
    return item[1]


def _push_family(family, z, sc, max_keep=12):
    if z is None:
        return family
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return family
    family.append((np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).copy(), float(sc)))
    family.sort(key=_element_score)
    if len(family) > max_keep:
        del family[max_keep:]
    return family


def _safe_evaluate(p):
    try:
        seq = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()]
        val = float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
        return val if np.isfinite(val) else np.inf
    except Exception:
        return np.inf


def _coarse_to_fine_path(p_start, final_n, deadline,
                         levels=(64, 128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048),
                         per_s=22.0, beta_factor=4.0):
    final_n = int(max(2, final_n))
    p = _normalize_to_sum(p_start, 1.0)
    if p.size == 0:
        p = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)

    wanted = [int(m) for m in levels if int(m) < final_n]
    wanted.append(final_n)
    wanted = sorted(set(x for x in wanted if x >= 2))
    if not wanted:
        p_final = _resample(p, final_n)
        z = _z_from_p(p_final)
        return z, _fast_score_z(z)

    p = _resample(p, wanted[0])
    best_z = None
    best_sc = np.inf

    for m in wanted:
        if time.monotonic() >= float(deadline):
            break
        p = _resample(p, m)
        z = _z_from_p(p)
        sub_deadline = min(float(deadline), time.monotonic() + float(per_s))
        z, _ = _run_lbfgs(z, sub_deadline, maxiter=80, beta_factor=float(beta_factor))
        p = _normalize_to_sum(_softmax(z), 1.0)

        p_final = _resample(p, final_n)
        zf = _z_from_p(p_final)
        sf = _fast_score_z(zf)
        if np.isfinite(sf) and sf < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sf)
            best_z = zf.copy()

    if best_z is None:
        p_final = _resample(p, final_n)
        best_z = _z_from_p(p_final)
        best_sc = _fast_score_z(best_z)

    return best_z, best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    """
    Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing
    evaluate_sequence.  Returns a list of floats.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    usable = max(60.0, float(budget_s) - 90.0)
    deadline = t0 + usable
    hard_limit = t0 + max(60.0, float(budget_s) - 5.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent_arr = None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if arr.size > 0 and float(arr.sum()) > 0.0:
            parent_arr = arr.astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    except Exception:
        parent_arr = None

    if parent_arr is not None:
        nparent = parent_arr.size
        n = min(8192, max(2048, nparent))
    else:
        n = 5582
    n = int(n)

    print(f"[main] starting, n={n}, parent={'yes' if parent_arr is not None else 'no'}")

    family = []
    profiles = _make_profiles(parent_arr, n, rng)
    for p in profiles:
        z = _z_from_p(p)
        sc = _fast_score_z(z)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            family = _push_family(family, z, sc, 16)

    if not family:
        uniform = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        z = _z_from_p(uniform)
        family = _push_family(family, z, _fast_score_z(z), 16)

    best_z = family[0][0].copy()
    best_sc = float(family[0][1])
    print(f"[main] best initial fast score = {best_sc:.9f}")

    # Short coarse-to-fine path(s).  These can escape shallow local minima.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 360.0:
        coarse_stop = min(deadline, t0 + max(180.0, 0.14 * usable))

        if parent_arr is not None:
            start_p = _resample(parent_arr, 64)
        else:
            start_p = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) / 64.0

        zctf, sctf = _coarse_to_fine_path(
            start_p, n, coarse_stop,
            levels=(64, 128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048),
            per_s=22.0, beta_factor=4.0
        )
        if np.isfinite(sctf):
            family = _push_family(family, zctf, sctf, 16)
            if family and family[0][1] < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(family[0][1])
                best_z = family[0][0].copy()
                print(f"[ctf] parent-based coarse-to-fine improved to {best_sc:.9f}")
        else:
            print("[ctf] parent-based coarse-to-fine no finite score")

        if time.monotonic() < coarse_stop - 120.0:
            rand_start = _z_from_p(np.maximum(np.exp(0.45 * rng.standard_normal(64)), 0.02))
            rand_z = _z_from_p(_softmax(rand_start))
            zctf2, sctf2 = _coarse_to_fine_path(
                _softmax(rand_z), n, coarse_stop,
                levels=(64, 128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048),
                per_s=20.0, beta_factor=4.0
            )
            if np.isfinite(sctf2):
                family = _push_family(family, zctf2, sctf2, 16)
                if family and family[0][1] < best_sc:
                    best_sc = float(family[0][1])
                    best_z = family[0][0].copy()
                    print(f"[ctf] random coarse-to-fine improved to {best_sc:.9f}")

    # Alternating full-resolution local refinements.
    gaps = [0.0, 0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.18, 0.30, 0.45, 0.70]
    lrs = [0.03, 0.05, 0.08, 0.12, 0.04, 0.07]
    betas = [4.0, 8.0, 14.0, 6.0, 10.0, 20.0]
    round_no = 0
    stagnant_rounds = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining < 70.0:
            break

        gap = gaps[round_no % len(gaps)]
        if gap == 0.0:
            base = best_z.copy()
        else:
            base = best_z + gap * rng.standard_normal(n)
            base -= float(np.mean(base))
            base = np.clip(base, -20.0, 20.0)

        chunk = min(140.0, max(25.0, 0.11 * remaining))
        sub_deadline = min(deadline - 30.0, time.monotonic() + chunk)

        if round_no == 0:
            zres, sres = _run_lbfgs(base, sub_deadline, maxiter=120,
                                    beta_factor=6.0, pgtol=1e-8)
        elif round_no % 2 == 1:
            zres, sres = _adam_refine(base, sub_deadline,
                                      lr=lrs[round_no % len(lrs)],
                                      beta_factor=betas[round_no % len(betas)],
                                      seed=seed + 1000 * round_no)
        else:
            zres, sres = _run_lbfgs(base, sub_deadline, maxiter=90,
                                    beta_factor=betas[round_no % len(betas)],
                                    pgtol=1e-8)

        improved = False
        if zres is not None and np.isfinite(sres) and sres < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = float(sres)
            best_z = np.asarray(zres, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            family = _push_family(family, best_z, best_sc, 16)
            improved = True
            stagnant_rounds = 0
            print(f"[fine][{round_no}] improved fast score to {best_sc:.9f} "
                  f"(gap={gap:.3f})")
        else:
            stagnant_rounds += 1
            if round_no % 4 == 0:
                print(f"[fine][{round_no}] no improvement (best={best_sc:.9f}, gap={gap:.3f})")

        round_no += 1

        if stagnant_rounds >= 4 and gap == 0.0:
            base = best_z + 0.15 * rng.standard_normal(n)
            base -= float(np.mean(base))
            base = np.clip(base, -20.0, 20.0)
            zres, sres = _adam_refine(base, min(deadline - 40.0, time.monotonic() + 60.0),
                                      lr=0.09, beta_factor=12.0,
                                      seed=seed + 5000 + round_no)
            if zres is not None and np.isfinite(sres) and sres < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_sc = float(sres)
                best_z = np.asarray(zres, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                family = _push_family(family, best_z, best_sc, 16)
                stagnant_rounds = 0
                print(f"[fine][{round_no}] perturbation improved to {best_sc:.9f}")

    # Final short polish.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 8.0:
        zpol, spol = _run_lbfgs(best_z, min(deadline - 2.0, time.monotonic() + 45.0),
                                maxiter=60, beta_factor=10.0, pgtol=1e-8)
        if zpol is not None and np.isfinite(spol) and spol < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = float(spol)
            best_z = np.asarray(zpol, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            print(f"[final polish] fast score improved to {best_sc:.9f}")

    # Verify finalists with the real evaluator.
    eval_candidates = []
    if best_z is not None:
        eval_candidates.append(best_z)
    for item in family[:4]:
        eval_candidates.append(item[0])
    eval_candidates.append(_z_from_p(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)))
    if parent_arr is not None:
        eval_candidates.append(_z_from_p(_resample(parent_arr, n)))

    if n > 7000:
        eval_candidates = eval_candidates[:3]
    elif n > 4000:
        eval_candidates = eval_candidates[:5]
    else:
        eval_candidates = eval_candidates[:8]

    best_actual = np.inf
    best_actual_p = None
    for z in eval_candidates:
        if time.monotonic() > hard_limit - 30.0:
            break
        p = _normalize_to_sum(_softmax(z), 1.0)
        actual = _safe_evaluate(p)
        print(f"[validate] actual score = {actual:.9f}")
        if np.isfinite(actual) and actual < best_actual:
            best_actual = actual
            best_actual_p = p

    if best_actual_p is None:
        uniform = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        val = _safe_evaluate(uniform)
        print(f"[validate] uniform fallback actual score = {val:.9f}")
        best_actual_p = uniform
        best_actual = val

    out = np.asarray(best_actual_p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    if out.size == 0:
        out = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
